===
Valid
===

cond(log,view=0,sqline) ; //DPV1693 (added view,sqline parameters)
	n buf,%classname,cond,funcsrc,i,%methodname,%mmm0co,mod,op,opflag,oper,string,%symtab,val4  //DPV1693, not new davals	// DPV3337 (%classname,%methodname,%symtax - used in LINES^%qarmac)
	s opflag=0,mod=0  ; set up stuff used in ptvalue^%qaqcpr
	If view=1 { d BuildFakeptiDD } //DPV1693=
	ElseIf view=-1 {}  // DPV1693-
	Else { d BuildFakepti }  //DPV1693-
	s string=$$$mtL(log)
#IF 0	; JLF158+
	i $e(string)="l" {
	        s op=$p(string,"^",2),val4=$$cond($p(string,"^",4),-1)
#ELSE	; JLF158=
	s op=$p($p(string,"^",2),"%NOINDEX") // PVA242
	#;  Generate the BuildValueArray loop for %ANYOF conditions
	#;
	n aoi,baseFld s baseFld=""
	f aoi=$ll($g($$$mtAnyOfVals(log))):-1:1 { if '$d(mt("anyofInProgress",$li($$$mtAnyOfVals(log),aoi))) { s baseFld=$li($$$mtAnyOfVals(log),aoi) quit }}
	if (baseFld="") && (op="ANYOFAND") 
	{	s op = "&&" }		// if not marked in mt("anyof",log), then just process it like a wide AND
	elseif (baseFld'="")
	{	QUIT $$genAnyof^%qaqcpre(log,baseFld,$s((op="ANYOFAND")&&(aoi=1):1,1:0),1) }
	i $e(string)="l" {	        
			s val4=$$cond($p(string,"^",4),-1,.sqline)	// DPV2947
#ENDIF	; JLF158-
	        }
	#; ------ Must be a comparison of 2 values ------
#IF 0	; JLF158+
	s op=$p(string,"^",2) s:op="!" op="||" s:op="&" op="&&"
#ENDIF	; JLF158-
#IF 1   ; DPV1693+ (AK505+ in %qaqcpr)
	QUIT:$e(op,1,4)="SET:" $$setval^%qaqcpr(val,1)
#ENDIF  ; DPV1693- (AK505- in %qaqcpr)
#IF 1   ; DPV1693+ (AK470+ in cond^%qaqcpr)
	  #;  set conditions:
	  #;  v^[']EMPTY^^s^  (not used for now)
	i op["EMPTY" QUIT "($d("_$$setval^%qaqcpr($p(string,"^",4),1)_")"_$s(op="EMPTY":"<",1:">")_"9)"
	  #;  v^ONEOF^^v^s^  - whether v is in the set s
	  #;  v^'ONEOF^^v^s^  - (not used for now, unclear whether needed)
	i op["ONEOF" QUIT $p(op,"ONEOF")_"$d("_$$adsub^%qaqcpr($$setval^%qaqcpr($p(string,"^",5),1),$$tomarkval^%qaqcpr($p(string,"^",4)))_")"
#ENDIF  ; DPV1693- (AK470- in cond^%qaqcpr)
	s val4=$$ptvalue^%qaqcpr($p(string,"^",4))
#IF 0   ; DPV1693+ (AK481+ in cond^%qaqcpr)
	QUIT:op["NULL" "("_val4_$s(op["'":"'",1:"")_"="""")"
#ELSE   ; DPV1693= (AK481= in cond^%qaqcpr)
	  #;  if collated, collate NULL
#IF 0	; DPV1799+
	QUIT:op["NULL" "("_val4_$s(op["'":"'",1:"")_"="""_$$colnul^%qaqcpr($p(string,"^",4))_""")"
#ELSE	; DPV1799=
	QUIT:op["NULL" "("_val4_$s(op["'":"'",1:"")_"="_$$colnul^%qaqcpr($p(string,"^",4),1)_")"
#ENDIF	; DPV1799-
#ENDIF  ; DPV1693- (AK481- in cond^%qaqcpr)
	  #;  method call - get code, generate subroutine, call with arguments
	i $e(op,1,7)="METHOD:" s op=$p(op,":",2) QUIT $$genmeth^%qaqcpr($p(op,".",1,$l(op,".")-1),$p(op,".",$l(op,".")),,$$genargs^%qaqcpr($p(string,"^",4,*)))
	  #;  extrinsic function call
	QUIT:$e(op,1,7)="EXTFUN:" "$$"_$TR($p(op,":",2),".","^")_"("_$$genargs^%qaqcpr($p(string,"^",4,*))_")"
	s cond=$$ptvalue^%qaqcpr($p(string,"^",5))
#IF 0   ; DPV1693+ (AK470+ in cond^%qaqcpr)
	  #;  correct to use ]] in all cases, numeric and non-numeric alike
	i op[">" QUIT "("_val4_$e("'",op["'")_"]]"_cond_")"
	i op["<" QUIT "("_cond_$e("'",op["'")_"]]"_val4_")"
	i op="%PATTERN" s op=$$$mtV($p(string,"^",5)) q "("_val4_"?"_$S($E(op)="t":$p(op,"^",2,$$$MaxRtnLineLength),1:"@("_cond_")")_")"
	i op="%STARTSWITH" QUIT $$swex^%qaqcpr(val4,cond)
	QUIT:op'="IN" "("_val4_op_cond_")"
#ELSE   ; DPV1693= (AK470= in cond^%qaqcpr)
	i op="%PATTERN" s op=$$$mtV($p(string,"^",5)) q "("_val4_"?"_$S($E(op)="t":$p(op,"^",2,$$$MaxRtnLineLength),1:"@("_cond_")")_")"
	QUIT:op'="IN" $$condop^%qaqcpr(val4,op,cond)
#ENDIF  ; DPV1693- (AK470- in cond^%qaqcpr)
	s cond="("_val4_"="_cond_")"
	f i=6:1:$l(string,"^")-1 s buf="("_val4_"="_$$ptvalue^%qaqcpr($p(string,"^",i))_")",cond=cond_"||"_buf
	QUIT "("_cond_")"
 
BuildFakepti	; Given mt("v"), build a fake pti() which contains the text we want returned for
	#;      each field, constant or variable.
	n s,val s val="" 
	i '$d(th) n th s th=0  //DPV1693=
	f  s val=$o($$$mtV(val),1,s) q:'val  d
	. i $e(s)="f" d  q
	.. n f,l s l=$p(s,"^",3),f=$p(s,"^",4)  ; field's label, name
	.. i l'="",l=$$$UPPER($$$TriggerNewName(th)) s pti(val)="{"_$$quoter2(f)_"*N}" q
	.. i l'="",l=$$$UPPER($$$TriggerOldName(th)) s pti(val)="{"_$$quoter2(f)_"*O}" q
	.. s pti(val)="{"_$$quoter2(f)_"}"
	. i $e(s)="t" s pti(val)=$p(s,"^",2) q:pti(val)=+pti(val)  s pti(val)=$$quoter(pti(val))
	. i $e(s)="x" s pti(val)=$p(s,"^",2)
	QUIT
 
BuildFakeptiDD	;  //DPV1693+
	#; Given mt("v"), build a fake pti() which contains the text we want returned for
	#;      each field, constant or variable to be used in the SQL filer
	n s,val,icol,vf,m,vloop s vloop="" 
#IF 1	; JLF015
	n ccol
#ENDIF	; JLF015
	For  {
		s vloop=$o($$$mtV(vloop),1,s) q:'vloop
		If $e(s)="f" {
			s icol=$$$smdGetIcolFromIfn(+$p(s,"^",3))	// this isn't working for collections
			s pti(vloop)="$g(%d("_icol_"))"
#IF 0	; JLF015			
			s:$p($$$mtV(vloop),"^",3)["_" pti(vloop)=$$gencoll^%qaqcpr($p($p($$$mtV(vloop),"^",3),"_",2),pti(vloop))
#ELSE	; JLF015
			s:(($p(s,"^",3)["_")&&($p($p(s,"^",3),"_",2)'["%ANYOF")) pti(vloop)=$$gencoll^%qaqcpre($p($p(s,"^",3),"_",2),pti(vloop))
 
			#; Ensure that the original field is always defined when an %ANYOF field is defined.
			#;
			if ($p(s,"^",3)["_%ANYOF")
			{	
				#; Create a new, settable %d variable for the collection element
				#;
				s pti(vloop)="%d("_icol_","_$i(ccol)_")" 
 
				s m = $$$mtRQb($$$mtR($p(s,"^",2)))
				s vf = $$nufld^%qaqpqo($p(s,"^",2),+$p(s,"^",3))
				if '$d(pti(vf))
				{	s icol=$$$smdGetIcolFromIfn(+$p($$$mtV(vf),"^",3))
					s pti(vf)="%d("_icol_")"		// the base column for the collection
				}
			}
#ENDIF	; JLF015 
		}
		ElseIf $e(s)="t" { s pti(vloop)=$p(s,"^",2) s:pti(vloop)'=+pti(vloop) pti(vloop)=$$quoter(pti(vloop)) }
		ElseIf $e(s)="x" { s pti(vloop)=$p(s,"^",2) }
		ElseIf $e(s)="v" { s pti(vloop)="%mmm0co"_$i(%mmm0co) }
	}
	QUIT  //DPV1693-
 
ReplaceNameInSQL(sqlcode,name,newold)	;
		#;  Given a string of SQL statement(s), find all occurances of <name>.Field
		#;  And change it to {Field*<newold>}
		#;  I'll do this by using the lexical analyser on the code and replacing the tokens found.
	n (callname,%delid,%compOptions,%compTags,%context,GDBMS69,I,II,lambda,name,newold,rtname,sqlcode,V) ; avoid any problems overwriting variables
	m input=sqlcode
	s (line,more,f,p)=0,sql=input,$$$mtPActiveQbs="",%mmmNo28Check=1  ; %mmmNoSQLCheck will suppress -28 errors
	s name=$$$UPPER(name)
	f  {
		d lext  
		q:token=$c(1)  // end of statement
		If $g(value)=name {
			s r1=f,savesql=sql d lext 
			If token="." {
				d lext 
				If p-1>r1 { s replace=$e(sql,r1,p-1) }  // same SQL line
				Else { s replace=$$$StripTrailingWhiteSpace($e(savesql,r1,*)) } // SQL scanned into next line
				s with=":{"_lower_"*"_newold_"}" 
				s repwith($l(replace),replace)=with  // Build a list of strings to replace
			}
		}
	}
		#;  We replace all the longest string first in case you have inserted.A replaced then inserted.AB.
		#;  This way inserted.AB gets replaced properly
	s i="" 
	f  {
		s i=$o(repwith(i),-1)
		q:i=""  //done with lengths
		s replace="" 
		f  {
			s replace=$o(repwith(i,replace),1,with)
			q:replace=""  // no more replace strings of this length
			f cnt=1:1:sqlcode s sqlcode(cnt)=$$strrep(sqlcode(cnt),replace,with,1)  // replace it
		}
	}
	QUIT
 
skipmany(t,c)	; Skip 't' token 'c' times
	f i=1:1:c d skip(t)
	QUIT
 
 
retcall	; Parse / Generate code for CALL Statement
	  #;
	  #;      [ :<host variable argument> = ] CALL <routine name> <SQL argument list>  [ USING <host variable> ]   (DPV3361 (USING)
	  #;
	  #;      <routine name> ::= <SQL method name>
	  #;              // Only for class methods projected as Stored Procedures
	  #;
	  #;      <SQL argument list> ::= ( [ <SQL argument> [ { , <SQL argument> } ... ] ] )
	  #;
	  #;      <SQL argument> ::= scalar_expression_argument   // Any expression which evaluates to a single value (scalx)
	  #;                      | :<host parameter argument>
	  #;
	  #;  If we come in through retcall, the procedure will return a value
	n returnvar
	d scalx^%qaqpslx() s returnvar=val	// DPV3244
	if info {
		new stuff
		set stuff=$g($$$mtV(val))
		if $e(stuff)="t" { set mtc(0,0,"call","param",0)=$lb("C",$p(stuff,"^",2)) }
		elseif stuff="x^?" { set mtc(0,0,"call","param",0)=$lb("?",val) }
		else { set:$e(stuff)="x" mtc(0,0,"call","param",0)=$lb("V",$p(stuff,"^",2)) }
	}
	d skip("="),skipid("CALL")
	g callcont
call	n returnvar set:info mtc(0,0,"call","param",0)=""
callcont	;
	n arglist,args,bindinputargs,bindoutputargs,byref,call,%class,data,default,execute,formalarg,formalspec,i,interface,jj,kind,lvars,m,member,membermethod,method,op,plow,pn,proc,procargs,procLow,pval,schema,schemaLow,stub,stuff,temparg,using
	s $$$mtStatementType="CAL",m=1,using=0
 
	  #;  Parse the PROCEDURE name
	d ParseIdentifier^%qaqpDDLParser(.pval,.plow,,1)
	s schema=$$$AllButLastPiece(pval,".")
	s:schema="_CURRENT_USER" schema=$$$UPPER($$$DefaultSchemaQ) //DPV1904
	s proc=$tr($$$LastPiece(pval,"."),$c(2),".")
	$$$MakeCall(pval,plow,call)
	$$$SetCallop(call,"call")
 
	  #;  Parse the parameters
	d skip("(") s args=0
	if '$$check(")") {
		f i=1:1 {
			if $$check(",") set:info mtc(0,0,"call","param",i)="" set args(i)="" continue  	// missing argument (DPV3396) 
			if token=")" set:info mtc(0,0,"call","param",i)="" set args(i)="" q  		// missing last argument (DPV3490) 
			d scalx^%qaqpslx(,1) 
			s args(i)=val,stuff=$g($$$mtV(val))
			if info {
				if $e(stuff)="t" { set mtc(0,0,"call","param",i)=$lb("C",$p(stuff,"^",2)) }
				elseif stuff="x^?" { set mtc(0,0,"call","param",i)=$lb("?",val) }
				else { set:$e(stuff)="x" mtc(0,0,"call","param",i)=$lb("V",$p(stuff,"^",2)) }
			}
			q:'$$check(",")  
		} 
		d skip(")")
		s args=i
	}

	set jj="",pn=0  ;  reassign the sorted parameters   (DPV3361)
	for {
		set jj=$o($$$mtRecordParams(jj)) q:jj=""
		set val=$$$mtRecordParams(jj),name=$e($$$mtV(val),3,*),pn=pn+1
			#; DLP2250 - add %msqlvarcparm. If it is defined, use it for the prefix for ? parameter names. Do not use
			#; subscripted parameters.
#if 0 ; DLP3117+
		set:name="?" name=$s($get(%msqlvarcparm)="":"%parm("_pn_")",1:%msqlvarcparm_pn),$$$mtV(val)="x^"_name  ;  dynamic
#else ; DLP3117=
		set:name="?" name=$s($get(%msqlvarcparm)="":"%parm("_pn_")",$e(%msqlvarcparm,*)'="(":%msqlvarcparm_pn,1:%msqlvarcparm_pn_")"),$$$mtV(val)="x^"_name  ;  dynamic
#endif ; DLP3117-
		if info {
			s mtc(0,0,"parameter","val",val)=pn,mtc(0,0,"parameter",pn)=name_$c(1)_val
			s i="" f  s i=$o(mtc(0,0,"call","param",i),1,data) q:i=""  if $lg(data)="?",val=$lg(data,2) set $li(mtc(0,0,"call","param",i),2)=pn q
		}
	}

	if $$check("USING") {	// DPV3361
		do var^%qaqpop $$$SetCallContextObject(call,val)
		set using=val
		s pti(val)=$p($$$mtV(val),"^",2)
	}
	//  Since we're evaluating multiple expressions in a common context
	//  we won't need to code generate the same expresson more than once
	n cgc d cgcInit^%qaqcasl()  ;  TRW1449;AK1090
	if 'info,args {
		n datag,l,lines,mod,newvars,opflag,s,sqline,tag,tagpre,val
		s val="",newvars="",tagpre=gtag,datag=1
		i '$d(%substk) n %substk s %substk=1
		For  {
			s val=$o($$$mtV(val),1,s) q:'val
			i $e(s)="t" s pti(val)=$p(s,"^",2) i pti(val)'=+pti(val) s pti(val)=$$quoter(pti(val))
			i $e(s)="x" s pti(val)=$p(s,"^",2)
			i $e(s)="v" {
				if %msqlvar="i%%" {
					set pti(val)=$$addcursorvar^%qaqcasl(%classname,"",val)
				} else {
					set pti(val)=$s(%msqlvar["(":$p(%msqlvar,"(")_"d"_"("_$p(%msqlvar,"(",2)_","_val_")",$$$%msqlvarIsLocalVar:%sqldatavar_val_"d",1:%msqlvar_"d"_"("_val_")")
					set newvars($p(pti(val),"("))=""
				}
			}
		}
		s opflag=0,tag="",lines=0,mod=1
		f i=1:1:args s:args(i)'="" args(i)=$$ptvalue^%qaqcpr(args(i))	// DPV3511
		If $d($$$sqline(mod,lines)) {
			s l=""
			i $d(newvars)>1 {
				s i="" f  s i=$o(newvars(i)) q:i=""  s newvars=newvars_i_","
				$$$SetCallCode(call,"New "_$e(newvars,1,*-1))
			}
			f  s l=$o($$$sqline(mod,l)) q:l=""  $$$SetCallCode(call,$$$sqline(mod,l))
		}
	}
	s:$g(returnvar)'="" pti(returnvar)=$p($$$mtV(returnvar),"^",2)		// DPV3361
 
	  #;  Look up Procedure Name and get the class
	set %class=$g($$$PROCclass($$$pPROC,schema,proc))
	if %class="" { do error(428) } ; Stored Procedure not found
		// If we are not allowed to call any class query as a proc, make sure the query is defined as SqlProc
		// $SYSTEM.Context.SQLCompile().Options("QueryAsProcOK2")=1 may be defined in %SQL.Statement.%PrepareClassQuery based on the flags pased into the method
	if '$g($SYSTEM.Context.SQLCompile().Options("QueryAsProcOK")),'$Get($$$PROCprojected($$$pPROC,schema,proc)) { do error(428) } ; Stored Procedure not found if query is not projected
		#; Record the class timestamp in the tClassTS variable
		#; if there is no class timestamp, the class has either been deleted or is in the middle of a compile, so report an error and let the caller to the SQL compiler deal with it
	new %ts1
	set %ts1=$g($$$comClassKeyRaw(%class,$$$cCLASStimechanged))
	if %ts1="" {
		for i=1:1:$$$LockTimeout {
			set %ts1=$g($$$comClassKeyRaw(%class,$$$cCLASStimechanged))
				#; if on the first try the timestamp is not defined, attempt to lock the class with a timeout of 0.  If we get the lock, the class is not currently being compiled and
				#; we have no idea if/when the class will be compiled.  Just release the lock immediately and report the error.
				#; We only wait on the timestamp if the class is locked, indicating it is being compiled.
			if i=1,%ts1="" {
				lock +^oddDEF(%class)#"S":0 
				if '$test { /* OK, it is locked, assume it is being compiled and wait for completetion or lock timeout */ } 
				else { lock -^oddDEF(%class)#"SI" quit  }	// class is not locked, quit loop and fall through to error 150b below
			}
			quit:%ts1'=""
			hang 1
		}
	}
	do:%ts1="" error("150b")	// Error message uses variable %class
	
	i info {	// DPV3022=
		s schemaLow=$g($$$PROCschema($$$pPROC,schema,proc))
		s procLow=$g($$$PROCname($$$pPROC,schema,proc))
		s mtc(0,0,"execute privs")=$g(mtc(0,0,"execute privs"))_$lb($lb(schemaLow,procLow,%class))
		s mtc(0,0,"call","schema")=schema,mtc(0,0,"call","proc")=proc
		set:using mtc(0,0,"call","using")=$$ptvalue^%qaqcpr(using)
	}
 
	  #;  Make sure the procedure is a class method and not a query
	#; DPV3361 i $g($$$PROCtype($$$pPROC,schema,proc))="query" d error(370)
 
	  #;  Make sure if the call is expecting a return value, the procedure actually returns a value
	i $g(returnvar)'="",'$g($$$PROCreturnval($$$pPROC,schema,proc)) d error(371)
 
	  #;  And get the procedures method
	s method=$g($$$PROCprocedure($$$pPROC,schema,proc))
	s interface = $$$PROCinterface($$$pPROC,schema,proc)
	if interface '= 1 {
		$$$comMemberKeyGetLvar(stub,%class,$$$cCLASSmethod,method,$$$cMETHstub)
		if stub = "" { $$$comMemberKeyGetLvar(formalspec,%class,$$$cCLASSmethod,method,$$$cMETHformalspecparsed) }
		else {
			set member = $p(stub,".",1), membermethod=$p(stub,".",2),kind=$p(stub,".",3)
			$$$comSubMemberKeyGetLvar(formalspec,%class,kind,member,$$$cCLASSmethod,membermethod,$$$cMETHformalspecparsed)
		}
	}
	else { $$$comMemberKeyGetLvar(formalspec,%class,$$$cCLASSquery,method,$$$cQUERYformalspecparsed) }
	new %ts2 set %ts2=$g($$$comClassKeyRaw(%class,$$$cCLASStimechanged))
	do:(%ts1'=%ts2) error("150a")	// Error message uses variable %class,%ts1,%ts2

	  #;  Build arguments
	set procargs=$ll(formalspec) if args>procargs d error(370)
 	s arglist="", bindinputargs = "", lvars = "", bindoutputargs = ""
 	If args {
		  #;  If the parameter is INOUT or OUT and also is a valid variable name, prepend the '.' so it is passed by reference
		f i=1:1:args {
			if $$PropertyOrArray(args(i)) {	// might be oref.prop or array reference, bind to temp variable, then merge back (if output)
				s formalarg = $List(formalspec,i), byref = $Case($List(formalarg,3), "":0, "&":1, "*":2, ".":1, :0), default = $List(formalarg,4)
					#; if I-O or OUTPUT then pass by reference.
				set temparg = "%qpar"_i, lvars = lvars _ "," _ temparg
				set bindinputargs = bindinputargs _ "," _ temparg _ "=" _ args(i)
				set arglist=arglist_",." _ temparg
				set:byref bindoutputargs = bindoutputargs _ "," _ args(i) _ "=" _ temparg
			}
				#; if the argument is not a valid local variable then don't care how it is passed because no output binding is possible.
			elseif '$zname(args(i),0) { s arglist = arglist _ "," _ args(i) }
			else {	// valid local variable name
				set formalarg = $List(formalspec,i), byref = $Case($List(formalarg,3), "":0, "&":1, "*":2, ".":1, :0), default = $List(formalarg,4)
					#; if I-O or OUTPUT then pass by reference.
				if byref { s arglist = arglist _ ",." _ args(i) }
					#; if INPUT argument, preserve undefindness and prevent any changing of the argument in the procedure code from being returned to the caller
				else {
					set temparg = "%qpar"_i, lvars = lvars _ "," _ temparg
					set bindinputargs = bindinputargs _ "," _ temparg _ "=" _ args(i)
					set arglist=arglist_",." _ temparg
				}
			}
		} // DPV2045 (Handle OUT Parameters)
		if bindinputargs '= "" { set bindinputargs = "m "_$e(bindinputargs,2,*)_" " }
		if bindoutputargs '= "" { set bindoutputargs = "m "_$e(bindoutputargs,2,*) }
	}
	if interface '= 1 { s execute = "$classmethod("""_%class_""","""_method_""""_arglist_")" }
	else { 
		set lvars = lvars_",%qHandle,%qCallStatus"
		set execute = "set %qCallStatus=$classmethod("""_%class_""","""_method_"Execute"",.%qHandle"_arglist_") if '%qCallStatus { set SQLCODE=-400,%sqlcontext.%SQLCODE=SQLCODE,%msg=$$GetErrorText^%apiOBJ(%qCallStatus),%sqlcontext.%Message=%msg quit  } do %sqlcontext.AddResultSet(##class(%SQL.ClassQueryResultSet).%New("""_%class_""","""_method_""",.%qHandle))"
	}
	  #;  Build the call
	if $g(returnvar)="" { $$$SetCallCode(call,bindinputargs_$s(interface=1:" ",1:"do ")_execute_" "_bindoutputargs) }
	else {	// DPV3361
		s:returnvar returnvar=$$ptvalue^%qaqcpr(returnvar)
		$$$SetCallCode(call,bindinputargs_"s "_returnvar_"="_execute_" "_bindoutputargs)
	}
	$$$SetCalllvars(call,lvars)
	QUIT
	
PropertyOrArray(val) [] {
		#; if the val is of the form oref.property, oref.property(...), i%property, r%property, or array(...), return 1; otherwise 0
	if ($e(val,1,2)="i%"||($e(val,1,2)="r%"))&&($zname($p($e(val,3,*),"("),0)) QUIT 1  // i%prop or r%prop with option (...)
	if val["(" {
		set pre=$p(val,"(")
		QUIT:$zname(pre,0) 1  	// array()
		if pre[".",$zname($p(pre,"."),0),$zname($p(pre,".",2),0) QUIT 1	// oref.property(...)
		QUIT 0	// something not a "variable"
	}
	if val[".",$zname($p(val,"."),0),$zname($p(val,".",2),0) QUIT 1	// oref.property
	QUIT 0	// something not a "variable"
}
 
CutCOSCode(COScode) ;
	#;  We know where the COS code resides withing the SQL statement, cut it out into the COScode array
	#;  Requires global variables used by the parser:
	#;         f, input, l1, line, m1, sql
	#;  COScode is passed by reference
	#;
	n code,i,indent,indentstr,l2,label,m2 s COScode=0  // DPV1694 - NEW code
	s l2=line,m2=f-1  ;  f = <beginning of next token>
#IF 0	; DPV1659+
	i l1=l2 s COScode=1,COScode(1)=$e(sql,m1+$s(input:$l(sql)-$l(input(l1)),1:0),m2) QUIT  ; COS is a single line
	s m2=m2-$l(sql)+$l(input(l2))  ; compensate for previous line overflow
#ELSE	; DPV1659=
	s m2=m2-$l(sql)+$l($g(input(l2)))  ; compensate for previous line overflow	// DPV2714 input(l2) may not be defined if statement ends with comments or blank lines)
	i l1=l2 {  // COS is a single line
		s code=$e(input(l1),m1,m2)
		i 'info,$e($zstrip(code,"<W"))=":" s code=$p(code,":",2,*) s label=1
		e  s label=0
		s COScode=1,COScode(1)=$e($c(9),'label)_code
		QUIT
	}
#ENDIF	; DPV1659-
	     #; end of last token may be a few lines up, as in "select a  |from| b"
	f  q:m2>0  s l2=l2-1,m2=m2+$l(input(l2)) f m2=m2:-1 q:$e(input(l2),m2)'=" "
	     #;  extract:  from input(l1) position m1, to input(l2) position m2
	     #;  Well try to format this the same as the user defined - using m1-1 as base0 for indentation
#IF 0	; DPV1659+
	s indent=m1-1
	s i=0 f i=l1:1:l2 d
	     #;  If the line begins with a label, remove the ':' before the label and all pre-whitespace
	     #;  (But only when we are compiling for real - not during info compiling because we are changing
	     #;   the input text for the statement)
	. i 'info,$e($zstrip(input(i),"<W"))=":" s input(i)=$p(input(i),":",2,*) s m1=1,label=1
	. e  s label=0,m1=1 f j=1:1:indent q:'($e(input(i),j)=" "||($e(input(i),j)=$c(9)))  s m1=m1+1
	. s COScode($i(COScode))=$e($c(9),'label)_$e(input(i),m1,$s(i=l2:m2,1:$$$MaxStringLength)) s m1=1
#ELSE	; DPV1659=
	s indent=0 f i=m1-1:-1:1 q:$e(input(l1),i)'=" "&&($e(input(l1),i)'=$c(9))  s indent=indent+1
	f i=l1:1:l2 {
		   #;  If the line begins with a label, remove the ':' before the label and all pre-whitespace
		   #;  (But only when we are compiling for real - not during info compiling because we are changing
		   #;   the input text for the statement)
		If l1=i     { s code=$e(input(i),m1,*) }
		ElseIf l2=i { s code=$e(input(i),1,m2) }
		Else        { s code=input(i) }
		If 'info,$e($zstrip(code,"<W"))=":" { s code=$p(code,":",2,*) s label=1,m1=1 }
		Else { s label=0,m1=1 f j=1:1:indent q:'($e(code,j)=" "||($e(code,j)=$c(9)))  s m1=m1+1 }
		s COScode($i(COScode))=$e($c(9),'label)_$e(code,m1,*)
	}
#ENDIF	; DPV1659-
	QUIT
 
 
user	//	CREATE/ALTER/DROP a user
	// DROP USER [ IF EXISTS ] <ident> [CASCADE] 
	// 
	// CREATE USER <ident> [ <password clause> ] [ DEFAULT <ident> <ident>] [ TEMPORARY <ident> <ident>] 
	// 
	// ALTER USER <ident> [ <password clause> ] [ DEFAULT <ident> <ident>] [ TEMPORARY <ident> <ident>] 
	// 
	// <password clause> ::= <pwprefix> <password>
	// <pwprefix> ::= IDENTIFY BY
	//                | IDENTIFIED BY
	// 				  | [ WITH ] PASSWORD

	new pwSpecified,userhndl,userop
	$$$MakeUser(value,lower,userhndl)                   		##; Create User Handle
	set userop = $case($$$mtStatementType,
		"CRUS" : "create user",
		"ALUS" : "alter user",
		       : "drop user"
	)
	$$$SetUserOperation(userhndl,userop)

	if $$$UserOperation(userhndl)="drop user" {					// DROP USER
		if $$checkid("IF") { do skip("EXISTS") set tChkExists=1 }
		$$$SetUserName(userhndl,lower) do skip("ID")
		$$$SetUserDropIfExists(userhndl,$get(tChkExists))
		$$$SetUserDropCascade(userhndl,$$check("CASCADE"))

	} else {													// CREATE USER or ALTER USER
		$$$SetUserName(userhndl,lower)
		do skip("ID")
		do parsePassword
		if $$check("DEFAULT")   { do skip("ID"),skip("ID") }	##; Oracle def tabspace
		if $$check("TEMPORARY") { do skip("ID"),skip("ID") }	##; Oracle tmp tabspace
		for {													##; Skip any Oracle QUOTA clauses
			quit:'$$checkid("QUOTA")
			do skip("ID"),skip("ON"),skip("ID")
		}
	}
	RETURN

parsePassword
	new pwSpecified
	set pwSpecified=0
	if $$checkid("IDENTIFY") {
		do skip("BY") 
		set pwSpecified=1
	} elseif $$checkid("IDENTIFIED") {
		do skip("BY") 
		set pwSpecified=1
	} elseif  $$check("WITH") {
		do skipid("PASSWORD")
		set pwSpecified=1
	} elseif $$checkid("PASSWORD") {
		set pwSpecified=1
	}
	if pwSpecified {
		if token="NULL" {	##; No Password
			$$$SetUserPassword(userhndl,"")
			do skip("NULL")
		} elseif token=9 {	##; Password is a numeric literal value		// DPV2983
			$$$SetUserPassword(userhndl,value)
			do skip(token)
		} else {			##; Password is identifier					// DPV2983
			$$$SetUserPassword(userhndl,lower)
			d skip(token)
		}
	} else {
			$$$SetUserPassword(userhndl,"")
	}
	RETURN
 
OraclePhysicalAttributesClause(op)	;
	  #;  Oracle specific parse-n-toss for when %DDLMode = "Oracle"
	If op="PCTFREE"||(op="PCTUSED")||(op="INITRANS")||(op="MAXTRANS")||(op="TABLESPACE") { d lext }
	ElseIf op="STORAGE" {
		d skip("(")
		For {
			If $$anyid(" INITIAL NEXT MINEXTENTS MAXEXTENTS PCTINCREASE FREELISTS FREELIST OPTIMAL BUFFER_POOL ",.op) {
				If op="INITIAL"||(op="NEXT") {
					d skip(9) d:'$$checkid("K") checkid("M")
				}
				If op="MINEXTENTS"||(op="PCTINCREASE")||(op="FREELISTS")||(op="FREELIST") {
					d:op="FREELIST" skipid("GROUPS")
					d skip(9)
				}
				If op="MAXEXTENTS" {
					d:'$$checkid("UNLIMITED") skip(9)
				}
				If op="OPTIMAL" {
					If '$$check("NULL") { d skip(9) d:'$$checkid("K") checkid("M") }
				}
				If op="BUFFER_POOL" {
					If '$$checkid("KEEP") If '$$checkid("RECYCLE") d skip("DEFAULT")
				}
			}
			Else { Quit  }
		}
		d skip(")")
	}
	QUIT
	
OracleParallelClause(op)	;
	  #;  Oracle specific parse-n-toss for %DDLMode = "Oracle"
	If op="NOPARALLEL" { QUIT  }
	ElseIf op="PARALLEL" {
		d skip("(")
		For {
			If $$anyid(" DEGREE INSTANCES ",.op) {
				d:'$$check("DEFAULT") skip(9)
			}
			Else { Quit  }
		}
		d skip(")")
	}
	QUIT
	
 
option	; SET OPTION ...
	new op
	do skip("OPTION")
	set info=0,mtc(0,0,"type")="SET OPTION"
	if $$checkid("COMPILEMODE") {
		do skip("=")
		if $$check("DEFERRED") { set mt("set option")=$lb("COMPILEMODE","DEFERRED") }
		elseif $$check("IMMEDIATE") { set mt("set option")=$lb("COMPILEMODE","IMMEDIATE") }
		elseif $$checkid("INSTALL") { set mt("set option")=$lb("COMPILEMODE","INSTALL") }
		elseif $$checkid("NOCHECK") { set mt("set option")=$lb("COMPILEMODE","NOCHECK") }
		else { new t set t="DEFERRED, IMMEDIATE, INSTALL, or NOCHECK" do error(1) }
		RETURN
	}
		#; positive integer value options
	if $$anyid(" AUTO_PARALLEL_THRESHOLD LOCK_TIMEOUT LOCK_ESCALATION_THRESHOLD ",.op) {
		do skip("=")
		set mt("set option")=$lb(op,value)
		if '$isvalidnum(value,,0) { new t set t="<positive numeric literal value>" do error(1) } 
		do skip(9)
		RETURN
	}
		#; TRUE/FALSE options
	if $$anyid(" EXACT_DISTINCT PKEY_IS_IDKEY SUPPORT_DELIMITED_IDENTIFIERS ",.op) {
		do skip("=") 
		new v 
		if $$check("TRUE") { set v=1 } 
		elseif $$check("FALSE") { set v=0 } 
		else { new t set t="TRUE or FALSE" do error(1) } 
		set mt("set option")=$lb(op,v)
		RETURN
	}
	if $$checkid("DEFAULT_SCHEMA") {
		do skip("=")
		new v
		set v=lower
		do skip("ID")
		while $$check("/") {
			set v=v_"/"_lower
			do skip("ID")
		}
		set mt("set option")=$lb("DEFAULT_SCHEMA",v)
		RETURN
	}

		#;  Now check for any Locale properties...
	if $$checkid("AM")						{ do skip("=") set mt("set option")=$lb("AM",value) do skip(9) RETURN }
	if $$checkid("DATE_FORMAT")				{ do skip("=") set mt("set option")=$lb("DATE_FORMAT",value) do skip(9) RETURN }
	if $$checkid("DATE_MAXIMUM")			{ do skip("=") set mt("set option")=$lb("DATE_MAXIMUM",value) do skip(9) RETURN }
	if $$checkid("DATE_MINIMUM")			{ do skip("=") set mt("set option")=$lb("DATE_MINIMUM",value) do skip(9) RETURN }
	if $$checkid("DATE_SEPARATOR")			{ do skip("=") set mt("set option")=$lb("DATE_SEPARATOR",value) do skip(9) RETURN }
	if $$checkid("DECIMAL_SEPARATOR")		{ do skip("=") set mt("set option")=$lb("DECIMAL_SEPARATOR",value) do skip(9) RETURN }
	if $$checkid("MIDNIGHT")				{ do skip("=") set mt("set option")=$lb("MIDNIGHT",value) do skip(9) RETURN }
	if $$checkid("MINUS_SIGN")				{ do skip("=") set mt("set option")=$lb("MINUS_SIGN",value) do skip(9) RETURN }
	if $$checkid("MONTH_ABBR")				{ do skip("=") set mt("set option")=$lb("MONTH_ABBR",value) do skip(9) RETURN }
	if $$checkid("MONTH_NAME")				{ do skip("=") set mt("set option")=$lb("MONTH_NAME",value) do skip(9) RETURN }
	if $$checkid("NOON")					{ do skip("=") set mt("set option")=$lb("NOON",value) do skip(9) RETURN }
	if $$checkid("NUMERIC_GROUP_SEPARATOR")	{ do skip("=") set mt("set option")=$lb("NUMERIC_GROUP_SEPARATOR",value) do skip(9) RETURN }
	if $$checkid("NUMERIC_GROUP_SIZE")		{ do skip("=") set mt("set option")=$lb("NUMERIC_GROUP_SIZE",value) do skip(9) RETURN }
	if $$checkid("PM")						{ do skip("=") set mt("set option")=$lb("PM",value) do skip(9) RETURN }
	if $$checkid("PLUS_SIGN")				{ do skip("=") set mt("set option")=$lb("PLUS_SIGN",value) do skip(9) RETURN }
	if $$checkid("TIME_FORMAT")				{ do skip("=") set mt("set option")=$lb("TIME_FORMAT",value) do skip(9) RETURN }
	if $$checkid("TIME_PRECISION")			{ do skip("=") set mt("set option")=$lb("TIME_PRECISION",value) do skip(9) RETURN }
	if $$checkid("TIME_SEPARATOR")			{ do skip("=") set mt("set option")=$lb("TIME_SEPARATOR",value) do skip(9) RETURN }
	if $$checkid("WEEKDAY_ABBR")			{ do skip("=") set mt("set option")=$lb("WEEKDAY_ABBR",value) do skip(9) RETURN }
	if $$checkid("WEEKDAY_NAME")			{ do skip("=") set mt("set option")=$lb("WEEKDAY_NAME",value) do skip(9) RETURN }
	if $$checkid("YEAR_OPTION")				{ do skip("=") set mt("set option")=$lb("YEAR_OPTION",value) do skip(9) RETURN }

	if $g(%DDLMode)="Oracle" {
		for { quit:token=$c(1)  do skip(token) }
	} else {
			#; If we get here, it is an invalid option, report an error
		new t set t="<valid option name>" do error(1)
	}
	RETURN

#INCLUDE %qaqplex
#INCLUDE %quoter
